A^T y ≥ 1 - ε q? (they c includes -ε q maybe) y≥0. Since A^T y =? y length 2n-1. A_{i,j}=q_{i-j}. A^T y_j = ∑_i y_i q_{i-j} = (q * y)_j. Dual min M ∑ y_i s.t. q*y ≥1 (or ≥1-εq), y≥0. Build A efficiently:
```
def _build_A(q):
    n=q.size
    A = np.zeros((2*n-1,n), float)
    qr = q[::-1]
    for i in range(n-1):
        L=i+1
        A[i,:L]=qr[-L:]
    for i in range(n-1,2*n-1):
        start=i-n+1
        L=n-start
        A[i,start:start+L]=qr[:L]
    return A
```
This is O(n^2) memory and O(n^2) fill. For n=512, fine; for 1024, 2M fill each LP; okay maybe 0.02s. For 2048, 8M too heavy for LP; skip.

Could use `scipy.linalg.toeplitz`? It builds dense, maybe faster. But constraints are Toeplitz with first column q, first row? np.convolve matrix is Toeplitz matrix with first column? For linear conv of g with q? A g where (A g)_i = ∑_j q_{i-j} g_j. This is a Toeplitz matrix (constant along diagonals) with first column [0,...,0,q_{n-1}?] Not exactly; length 2n-1 columns n. `toeplitz` with first col/reversed. We can use loops; simple.

Could use sparse? A has n entries per row? Actually each row has up to n nonzeros, about n^2 total, so dense. But banded with bandwidth n, half dense. No sparsity advantage.

def _local_polish(q, deadline, rng, min_improve=1e-12):
    q = _normalize(q); n=q.size
    sc = _score_fast(q)
    best = q.copy(); best_sc = sc
    step = 0.05  # for multiplicative exponent (relative scale) maybe
    no_improve = 0
    max_sub = max(200, 20000//n?)
    while time < deadline-...:
        # compute b,M
        b,M = _conv_max(q)
        # active weights variants:
        directions = []
        # top threshold weights
        for alpha in (0.02,0.05,0.1):
             act = b >= M - alpha*M - 1e-14
             if act.size > 128: top128
             compute h
             directions.append((h, 'w'))
        # uniform top 8
        directions.append uniform top 8
        # maybe single max lag
        # for each direction:
             d = -h; center; scale = max(abs(d)); eta = d/scale; if norm too small continue
             try multiplicative alpha grid [0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.4,0.8,1.5?]. But if alpha=1.5, exp changes 350%, maybe okay but could destroy best. It can be accepted if score improves.
             also additive: cand = q + beta*(q*eta?)? Actually use q + beta*eta? If eta dimensionless signs, adding eta changes q by eta (O1) too large. Need scale to q. Use d0 = -h (O M~1/n). Make h:
def _h_from_b(b, M, q, max_k=64, uniform=False, thresh=0.05):
   n=q.size
   if M<=0: return None
   if b.size <= max_k:
      act=np.arange(b.size)
   else:
      act=np.where(b >= M - max(thresh*M, 1e-12))[0]
      if act.size > max_k:
          # top max_k
          tmp = np.argpartition(b[act], -max_k)[? Need indices. Use act[np.argpartition(-b[act], max_k-1)[:max_k]]
   w = np.ones(act.size)
   if not uniform and act.size>1:
       w = np.exp((b[act]-M)/max(M*0.1,1e-15))
   w /= w.sum()
   h=np.zeros(n)
   for i, ww in zip(act,w):
       st=max(0,i-n+1); en=min(n-1,i)
       # segment length en-st+1
       h[st:en+1] += 2.0*ww * q[i-st : i-en+1][::-1]
   return h

def _nonneg_normalize(x, floor=0):
    x=np.where(finite,x,0); x=np.maximum(x,0); s=x.sum()
    if s<=0: return np.full_like(x,1/x.size)
    return x/s

```
Let's define `_put_at_length(prev, k)`:
```
def _resize(q, k):
   if q is None or q.size==0: return uniform
   if q.size == k: return _normalize(q)
   if q.size < k:
       # linear interpolation, with small smoothing
       x_old=np.linspace(0,1,q.size); x_new=np.linspace(0,1,k)
       out=np.interp(x_new,x_old,q)
   else:
       # average blocks via interpolation down
       out=np.interp(np.linspace(0,1,k), np.linspace(0,1,q.size), q)
   out = np.maximum(out,0)
   # apply small smoothing to reduce aliasing? optional
   if out.size >=8:
       w=np.array([1,2,1]); w/=w.sum(); out=np.convolve(out,w,mode='same')
   return _normalize(out)
```
Smoothing may alter parent but okay. For upsampling, perhaps better not smooth? We can make two variants: plain interpolation and smoothed. In start generation include both.
```

def _make_starts(k, parent, best_q, rng, max_starts=3):
   starts=[]
   if parent is not None: starts.append(_resize(parent,k))
   if best_q is not None: starts.append(_resize(best_q,k))

```
Let q current sum1. Move mass δ from index a to b: q_a -= δ, q_b += δ, with δ in [-q_a, q_b/(?) actually can move negative for b to a]. New q nonnegative. Objective M(δ) = max_i (b_i + 2δ(q_i? terms) + δ^2? Let's derive: For each lag i, contribution terms involving a or b. If a≠b. b_i = ∑_{r+s=i} q_r q_s (ordered). Moving δ from a to b:
- If shift a→b (q_a = q_a - δ, q_b = q_b + δ). Difference in b_i:
   Let oa = ∑_r such that i-r=a? terms q_i? easier: pairs with a: coefficients. The change is linear plus δ^2 if both a and b paired in same i (when i=a+b or 2a etc).
For each i, Δ_i(δ):
   terms originally involve: q_a terms replaced by q_a-δ, q_b terms replaced q_b+δ.
   Let p_i = number/weight of pairs where not both a/b? Could compute Δ_i = -δ * R_i + δ * S_i + terms δ^2 when i=2a or i=2b or i=a+b? Specifically if a≠b:
   - term a+a (if 2a=i) changes (q_a-δ)^2 - q_a^2 = -2q_a δ + δ^2.
   - term b+b: +2q_b δ + δ^2.
   - terms a+b (if a+b=i): ordered pairs (a,b) and (b,a). Original 2 q_a q_b. New 2(q_a-δ)(q_b+δ)=2q_a q_b + 2δ(q_a - q_b) -2δ^2.
     So Δ = 2δ(q_a - q_b) -2δ^2.
   - terms with a and c (c not b): each pair (a,c) and (c,a) if 2a? Actually for fixed other c, pairs where one is a: q_a q_c; after q_a-δ: -δ q_c for each ordered occurrence. If c≠b, occurrence count maybe 1 or 2 depending if i=a+c = c+a? If c≠a, two ordered pairs (a,c) and (c,a) if both valid? In convolution sum over all j, term q_j q_{i-j}; both j=a and j=c appear if i=a+c. So original contribution 2 q_a q_c; change -2δ q_c (if c≠b? if c=b also handled a+b). Similarly b gains +2δ q_c. def consider(seq):
```
    nonlocal best_list, best_score
    if seq is None or time.monotonic() > deadline - 2: return
    arr = np.asarray(seq,dtype=float).ravel()
    if arr.size == 0: return
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0)
    arr = np.maximum(arr,0)
    s = arr.sum()
    if s <= 0.01? Normalized sum 1, okay.
    arr = arr/s
    if arr.max()>1000: arr=np.clip(arr,0,1000)
    lst=[float(x) for x in arr]
    val=float(evaluate_sequence(lst))
    if np.isfinite(val) and val < best_score:
       best_score=val; best_list=lst

Main local loop:

```
def _polish(q, best_sc, deadline, rng, max_iter=None):
    q=_normalize(q); q_sc=_score_fast(q); best_q=q.copy(); best_sc=min(best_sc,q_sc)
    no=0
    while time < deadline - 5 and no < max_no:
       step = _polish_step(q, q_sc, rng)
       if step is not None:
           q, q_sc = step
           if q_sc < best_sc - 1e-14:
               best_sc=q_sc; best_q=q.copy()
           no=0
       else:
           no +=1
           if no == 8 or no == 20 or no == 40:
               # random perturbation of best_q (not q? but q=best unless worse)
               pert = _perturb(best_q, 0.02/np.sqrt(n) * (no/10), rng)
               psc = _score_fast(pert)
               if psc < best_sc:
                  best_q=pert; best_sc=psc; q=pert; q_sc=psc; no=0; continue
               # We could set q=pert to explore, but step only accepts improvements from q_sc; if pert worse, q_sc worse.
               # Let's allow exploring: set q=pert, q_sc=psc, no=0, but if q_sc > best_sc + 1e-6 and no subsequent? We'll set explore_count.
               q=pert; q_sc=psc; no=0
           elif no >= 80: break
    return best_q, best_sc
```

Random perturbation:
```
def _perturb(q, scale, rng):
    n=q.size
    r = rng.standard_normal(n)
    # low-pass: zero or reduce high frequencies
    rhat = np.fft.rfft(r)
    keep = int(n*0.2)
    if keep<0: f? else rhat[keep:]*=0.2
    r = np.fft.irfft(rhat, n)
    r -= r.mean()
    rn = np.linalg.norm(r)
    if rn>0: r = r / rn * scale
    return _normalize(q + r)
```
Scale = maybe 0.01/np.sqrt(n). This changes relative O(1)?? r norm scale, entries O(scale/√n)? Actually after normalize r norm=scale; adding to q where q norm~1/√n, relative comparable if scale~1/√n. Use scale = 0.05/np.sqrt(n). Good.

```
def _optimize_start(q0, k, rng, end_time, use_lp):
    q = _normalize(q0); best_q=q.copy(); best_sc=_score_fast(q); q_sc=best_sc
    lp_stag=0; polish_stag=0; it=0
    # If use_lp, we will alternate: do LP after some polish? Let's do: first a short polish burst (until time+min(20,0.15 remaining)), then LP/polygon loop.
    # But maybe start parent already close; LP first.
    polish_burst = min(30, max(5, (end_time-time)*0.15)) if time<end_time else end_time
    q,best_sc,_ = _polish(q,best_sc,polish_burst,rng,max_no=20) # returns q? Need _polish returns best and current? It updates best_q, but q may be perturbed. We'll return best_q as current for next? Use best.
    q=best_q.copy(); q_sc=best_sc
    lp_counter=0; max_lp = 60 if k<=640 else 30 if k<=1024 else 0
    while time < end_time - 10:
        remaining = end_time - time
        # Decide do LP if use_lp and lp_counter<max_lp and not (remaining < 20)
        if use_lp and lp_counter<max_lp:
            # if LP stagnation high, switch to polish until another LP? maybe if lp_stag==3, do polish burst.
            if lp_stag < 4:
```

Detailed helper code:
```
import numpy as np
import time
try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog
except Exception:
    linprog = None

def _normalize(x, size=None):
    if size is None:
        x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float).ravel()
        if x.size == 0:
            return np.array([1.0])
    else:
        x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float).ravel()
    x = np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
    x = np.maximum(x, 0.0)
    s = float(x.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.full(max(1, x.size), 1.0/max(1,x.size), dtype=float)
    x = x / s
    return x
```
If x contains extremely small, okay.

```
if b is None: b=a
la=a.size; lb=b.size
out_len=la+lb-1
if out_len ==0: return np.array([])
# For exact search n<=2048 use np.convolve; but for very large start? no
if max(la,lb) <= 1536:
    return np.convolve(a,b, mode='full')
# FFT
L=1
while L < out_len: L <<=1
# next fast power2 okay
fa=np.fft.rfft(a,L); fb=np.fft.rfft(b,L)
c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fb,L)[:out_len]
# remove tiny negative? not necessary; but for max maybe negative small
return c.real
```

`_make_h` as above. Ensure `act` array.
```
def _make_h(q,b,M,mode='w',thresh=0.05,max_k=64,uniform=False):
    n=q.size
    if M <= 0: return None
    if mode=='single':
       act=np.array([int(np.argmax(b))])
    else:
       act=np.flatnonzero(b >= M - max(float(thresh)*M, 1e-14))
       if act.size > max_k:
           # To get top max_k by b
           part = np.argpartition(-b[act], max_k-1)[:max_k]
           act = act[part]
    if act.size <=0: return None
    if uniform:
       w=np.ones(act.size)
    else:
       tau=max(0.15*M,1e-15)
       w=np.exp((b[act]-M)/tau)
    ws=w.sum()
    if ws<=0: w=np.ones(act.size)
    else: w=w/ws
    h=np.zeros(n,float)
    for idx, ww in zip(act, w):
       i=int(idx)
       st=max(0,i-n+1); en=min(n-1,i)
       # if ww too small skip
       if ww <= 1e-14: continue
       seg = q[i-en : i-st+1][::-1]
       h[st:en+1] += (2.0*ww)*seg
    h -= h.mean()
    norm=np.max(np.abs(h))
    if not np.isfinite(norm) or norm <= 1e-15: return None
    return h / norm
```
Returns normalized eta direction? Actually h itself normalized to max 1, but we'll compute eta later. If h normalized to max1, additive alpha 1/n meaningful. Good.
But if h = h/norm, scale 1. Factor 2 irrelevant. Good.

Candidate generation with normalized eta:
```
def _candidate_from_eta(q, eta, kind, alpha):
    n=q.size
    if kind==0: # exp
       qs=np.maximum(q,1e-15)
       cand=qs*np.exp(alpha*eta)
    elif kind==1: # relative
       cand=q*(1.0+alpha*eta)
    else:
       cand=q + alpha*eta
    return _normalize(cand)
```
Alpha for additive now 1/n etc because eta O1. For relative alpha up to 0.8. For exp alpha up to 2? Since eta O1, exp(2) factor 7. Could be useful. Use [0.05,0.25,1.0,2.0] if n small; but 4. We'll use [0.05,0.25,1.0]. For large [0.05,1.0]. Relative [0.1,0.5]. Additive [1/n, 10/n]. If n large, 10/n small (0.005 for 2048). Good.

```
def _polish(q, best_sc, deadline, rng, max_no=30, perturb_once=True):
    q=_normalize(q); q_sc=_score_fast(q)
    if q_sc < best_sc: best_sc=q_sc; best_q=q.copy()
    else: best_q=q.copy()
    no=0; perturb=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 5 and no < max_no:
       step = _polish_step(q, q_sc)
       if step is not None:
          q, q_sc = step
          if q_sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
             best_sc=q_sc; best_q=q.copy()
          no=0
       else:
          no +=1
          if no == 10 and perturb < 2 and time.monotonic() < deadline - 20:
             pert = _perturb(best_q, 0.05/np.sqrt(q.size), rng)
             psc = _score_fast(pert)
             if psc < best_sc - 1e-12:
                 best_sc=psc; best_q=pert.copy(); q=pert; q_sc=psc; no=0; perturb+=1
             else:
                 # optionally set q=pert to explore but risk; skip, continue
                 q = best_q.copy(); q_sc=best_sc
          elif no == 20 and perturb < 4 and time < deadline - 20:
             pert = _perturb(best_q, 0.1/np.sqrt(q.size), rng)
             psc=...
             if psc < best_sc - 1e-12: update
             else: q=best_q; q_sc=best_sc
          # else continue? If no increases each loop, but step will fail again if q unchanged; break to avoid wasting.
          if no >= 12: break
    return best_q, best_sc
```

`_lp_response` exact? For n 1024, score fast uses FFT if threshold 1536? 1024 uses np.convolve. Good.
```
def _lp_response(q,rng,end_time):
   if linprog is None: return None
   n=q.size
   if n ==0 or n>1536: return None
   b=_convolve_full(q,q)
   M0=float(np.max(b))
   if not np.isfinite(M0) or M0<=0: return None
   qs=q*float(n)
   M=float(n)*M0
   A=_build_A(qs)
   c = -np.ones(n) - 1e-5*qs
   # add small random perturb to break degeneracy
   c = c + rng.normal(0.0, 1e-9, size=n)
   b_ub=np.full(n? 2*n-1, M)
   opts={'presolve': True}
   tlim = max(0.2, min(8.0, (end_time - time.monotonic()) - 5.0))
   if n > 768: tlim = max(0.5, min(10.0, tlim))
   try:
       res=linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=b_ub, bounds=(0.0, 1e6), method='highs', options={**opts,'time_limit':max(0.1,tlim)})
   except Exception:
       try: res=linprog(... without time_limit)
       except: return None
   if res is None or not res.success: return None
   g=np.asarray(res.x,float)
   g=np.where(np.isfinite(g)&(g>0),g,0)
   s=g.sum()
   if s<=0: return None
   g/=s
   return g
```
Time limit may be too small for 1024; but if fail return None -> no LP. We can try `method='highs'` without time limit if highS fails due timeout? But then could blow time. Maybe if n<=768 and tlim>5, okay. We can set time limit 15 for 1024. Budget large. Let's set:
```
tlim = max(0.5, min(20.0, (end_time - now) - 8.0))
if n <=640: tlim = min(8.0, tlim)
elif n <=1024: tlim = min(15.0, tlim)
```
If LP takes 15s per call, 10 calls=150s; okay.
If `res.success` but primal infeasible? return.

Potential HiGHS option `time_limit` in seconds maybe expects float. Good.

Build A for LP with scaled `qs`:
```
def _build_A(qs):
   n=qs.size
   A=np.zeros((2*n-1,n),float)
   qr=qs[::-1]
   for i in range(n-1):
      L=i+1
      A[i,:L]=qr[-L:]
   for i in range(n-1,2*n-1):
      start=i-n+1
      L=n-start
      A[i,start:start+L]=qr[:L]
   return A
```
This copies. For n=1024, loops 2047 assignments; okay.

Alternative: use `np.empty` and fill? zeros okay.

```
def _random_start(k, rng, variant=0):
    t = (np.arange(k)+0.5)/k
    raw = np.full(k,1.0)
    signs = []
    mmax = min(4, max(1,k//8?)) maybe 3
    for m in range(1,mmax+1):
       amp = 0.45/(m**0.5) if m>1 else 0.75
       ph = rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
       raw += amp*np.sin(2*np.pi*m*t + ph)
       raw += amp*0.7*np.cos(2*np.pi*m*t + rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi))
    raw = np.maximum(raw, 1e-3)
    # smooth
    return _normalize(raw)
```

```
def _resize(q,k,smooth=False):
   if q is None or q.size==0: return _normalize(np.linspace(0,1,k).astype?) no
   if q.size == k: out=q
   else:
      x_old=np.linspace(0,1,q.size)
      x_new=np.linspace(0,1,k)
      out=np.interp(x_new,x_old,q)
   if smooth and out.size >= 5:
      w=np.array([1.0,2.0,1.0]); w/=w.sum(); out=np.convolve(out,w,'same')
   return _normalize(out)
```

```
def _lp_refine(q,best_sc,rng,end_time,max_lp):
   q=_normalize(q); best_q=q.copy(); best_sc=_score_fast(q)
   stag=0
   for _ in range(max_lp):
      if time.monotonic() > end_time - 5: break
      g=_lp_response(q,rng,end_time)
      if g is None: break
      cands=_mix_candidates(q,g)
      cand_best=None; cand_sc=best_sc
      eps = 1e-11 if q.size>=1536 else 1e-12
      for c in cands:
         sc=_score_fast(c)
         if sc < cand_sc - eps:
            cand_sc=sc; cand_best=c
      if cand_best is not None and cand_sc < best_sc - eps:
          q=cand_best.copy(); best_sc=cand_sc; best_q=q.copy(); stag=0
      else:
          stag +=1
          if stag>=5: break
   return best_q,best_sc
```

`_conv_full`: If using np.convolve for exact for n<=1536, for larger FFT. But for LP at 1024 uses np.convolve. Good.
```
def _conv_full(a,b=None):
    a=np.asarray(a,float); b=a if b is None else np.asarray(b,float)
    la,lb=len(a),len(b)
    if la==0 or lb==0: return np.array([],float)
    out_len=la+lb-1
    if max(la,lb) <= 1536:
        return np.convolve(a,b,mode='full')
    L=1
    while L < out_len: L <<=1
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
    fb=np.fft.rfft(b,L)
    out=np.fft.irfft(fa*fb,L)[:out_len]
    return out.real
```
FFT length power of two, not next fast; okay.

`_score_fast`: If b length zero return inf. `m = float(np.max(b))`; if not finite or m<0: maybe 0. Score=2*n*m.

If act.size == max_k, no partition. Use:
```
if act.size > max_k:
    vals = -b[act]
    idx = np.argpartition(vals, max_k-1)[:max_k]
    act = act[idx]
```

Segment slice: `q[i-en : i-st+1][::-1]`. Need ensure start <= stop. i-en ≤ i-st. If i-en can be negative? i>=0, en<=n-1. Minimum i-en: if i=0,en=0 ->0. Max? If i<n-1, en=i, i-en=0. If i>=n-1, st=i-n+1≥0; en=n-1; i-en ≥ i-(n-1) = i-n+1=st≥0. Good. i-st+1 ≤ n? For i≤2n-2, st=max(0,i-n+1). If i<n, st=0, i-st+1=i+1≤n. If i≥n, st=i-n+1, i-st+1=n. Good.

`_candidates_for_eta`: qsafe for exp. If `alpha*eta` maybe zero. Normalize. We should ensure cand not None. `_normalize` always returns array.
```
def _candidates_for_eta(q, eta):
    n=q.size
    out=[]
    if n <= 768:
       exps=(0.05,0.25,1.0); rels=(0.1,0.5); adds=(1.0/n,10.0/n)
    elif n <= 1536:
       exps=(0.05,1.0); rels=(0.3,); adds=(1.0/n,5.0/n)
    else:
       exps=(0.05,1.0); rels=(); adds=(5.0/n,)
    qs = np.maximum(q, 1e-15)
    for a in exps:
        out.append(_normalize(qs * np.exp(a*eta)))
    for a in rels:
        out.append(_normalize(q * (1.0 + a*eta)))
    for a in adds:
        out.append(_normalize(q + a*eta))
    return out
```
If additive alpha eta has eta mean zero but values up to1; q+a*eta can negative; normalize clips. Good.

`_polish_step`: `eps = 1e-10 if n >=1536 else 1e-12`. Use `best_s = q_sc`; candidate accepted if sc < best_s - eps. If no candidate, None. We may evaluate 2-3 directions. To reduce recompute, if a candidate exactly same? ignore.
```
def _polish_step(q, q_sc):
    n=q.size
    b = _conv_full(q)
    M = float(np.max(b))
    if not np.isfinite(M) or M <=0: return None
    dirs=[]
    if n <= 1536:
       dirs.append(_make_h(q,b,M,uniform=False,thresh=0.05,max_k=64,single=False))
       dirs.append(_make_h(q,b,M,uniform=True,thresh=0.02,max_k=24,single=False))
    else:
       dirs.append(_make_h(q,b,M,uniform=True,thresh=0.03,max_k=16,single=False))
    dirs.append(_make_h(q,b,M,single=True))
    best_c=None; best_s=q_sc; eps=...
    for h in dirs:
       if h is None: continue
       for cand in _candidates_for_eta(q,h):
           sc = _score_fast(cand)
           if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_s - eps:
               best_s=sc; best_c=cand
    if best_c is not None:
       return best_c, best_s
    return None
```
If q_sc is not the best global but current worse, it finds small improvements. Good.

`_polish`: We need pass rng for perturb. `max_no` not really used if break on fail. We'll allow one or two perturb attempts.
```
def _polish(q,best_sc,deadline,rng,max_no=20):
    q=_normalize(q); q_sc=_score_fast(q)
    if q_sc < best_sc: best_sc=q_sc; best_q=q.copy()
    else: best_q=q.copy()
    attempts_perturb=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 5:
       step = _polish_step(q,q_sc)
       if step is not None:
          q,q_sc=step
          if q_sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
             best_sc=q_sc; best_q=q.copy()
          attempts_perturb=0
          continue
       if attempts_perturb < 2 and time.monotonic() < deadline - 20:
          scale = (0.05 if attempts_perturb==0 else 0.10)/np.sqrt(max(1,q.size))
          pert = _perturb(best_q, scale, rng)
          psc = _score_fast(pert)
          attempts_perturb +=1
          if psc < best_sc - 1e-12:
             best_sc=psc; best_q=pert.copy(); q=pert; q_sc=psc; continue
          # maybe set q = _resize? no
       break
    return best_q,best_sc
```
If first pert fails, second maybe. If both fail, break. This works.

`_perturb`: Need ensure if q.size=1 no FFT. Use norm.
```
def _perturb(q,scale,rng):
   n=q.size
   if n==0: return q
   r = rng.standard_normal(n)
   if n >=16:
      rf=np.fft.rfft(r)
      cut=max(2, int(n*0.15))
      rf[cut:]*=0.0
      r=np.fft.irfft(rf,n)
   r -= r.mean()
   rn = float(np.linalg.norm(r))
   if rn <= 1e-14:
      r = rng.standard_normal(n); r-=r.mean(); rn=float(np.linalg.norm(r))
      if rn <=1e-14: r = np.ones(n)-1/n
   r = r/rn*scale
   return _normalize(q + r)
```
For n small, no low pass.

```
def _smooth_array(q):
   q=np.asarray(q,float)
   if q.size <3: return q
   w = np.ones(min(5,q.size|1),float) # if even? Use 3.
   w = np.array([1,2,1]); w/=w.sum()
   return np.convolve(q,w,mode='same')
```

`_resize`: smooth optional.
```
def _resize(q,k,smooth=False):
   if q is None:
       return _normalize(np.ones(k))
   q=np.asarray(q,float).ravel()
   if q.size ==0 or q.size ==1:
       out = np.full(k, (q[0] if q.size else 1.0) / max(1,k))
       return _normalize(out)
   if q.size == k: out=q.copy()
   else:
       xo=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,q.size)
       xn=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,k)
       out=np.interp(xn,xo,q)
   if smooth and out.size >=3:
       w=np.array([1.0,2.0,1.0]); w/=w.sum()
       out=np.convolve(out,w,mode='same')
   return _normalize(out)
```
If q.size=1, out filled with q0/k? sum q0? _normalize makes uniform. Fine.

`_random_start`:
```
def _random_start(k,rng,variant=0):
    if k<=0: return _normalize(np.ones(1))
    t=(np.arange(k)+0.5)/float(k)
    raw=np.ones(k)
    mmax = min(3, max(1, k//16))
    for m in range(1,mmax+1):
       ph1 = rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
       ph2 = rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
       amp1 = 0.80 if m==1 else 0.45/(m**0.7)
       amp2 = 0.50 if m==1 else 0.35/(m**0.7)
       raw += amp1*np.sin(2*np.pi*m*t + ph1)
       raw += amp2*np.cos(2*np.pi*m*t + ph2)
    # variant-specific fixed skew:
    if variant % 2:
       raw += 0.8*np.sin(2*np.pi*t)
    else:
       raw -= 0.8*np.sin(2*np.pi*t)
    raw = np.maximum(raw, 1e-3)
    return _normalize(raw)
```
This may be too random; okay.

`_make_starts`:
```
def _make_starts(k,best_q,parent,rng,max_starts=3):
    starts=[]
    if best_q is not None:
       starts.append(_resize(best_q,k,False))
       starts.append(_resize(best_q,k,True))

       starts.append(_resize(parent,k,False))
       starts.append(_resize(parent,k,True))
    # deterministic sine starts
    t=(np.arange(k)+0.5)/float(k)
    for func in ...:
    # random
    for v in range(2): starts.append(_random_start(k,rng,v))
    # unique
    uniq=[]
    for q in starts:
       q=_normalize(q)
       if q is None: continue
       same=False
       for u in uniq:
          if np.max(np.abs(q-u)) < 1e-6:
             same=True; break
       if not same:
          uniq.append(q)
          if len(uniq) >= max_starts: break
    if not uniq: uniq=[_normalize(np.ones(k))]
    return uniq[:max_starts]
```

```
def propose_candidate(seed=42,budget_s=1300,**kwargs):
    seed=int(seed)
    try: budget_s=float(budget_s)
    except: budget_s=1300.0
    if np.isfinite(budget_s) and budget_s>30: hard = time.monotonic()+budget_s
    else: hard = time.monotonic()+max(5.0,budget_s)
    t0=time.monotonic()
    deadline = hard - max(15.0, 0.025*budget_s)
    if deadline < t0 + 2: deadline = t0 + max(1.0,budget_s*0.5)
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    best_list=None; best_score=float('inf'); best_arr=None
    def consider(seq):
       nonlocal best_list,best_score,best_arr
       if seq is None: return
       if time.monotonic() > deadline - 2 and best_list is not None: return
       arr=_normalize(np.asarray(seq,dtype=float).ravel())
       if arr is None or arr.size==0: return
       # clip to evaluator cap after normalize? normalize sum1 so no issue
       arr=np.clip(arr,0.0,1000.0)
       if arr.sum() <=0: return
       lst=[float(x) for x in arr]
       try:
          val=float(evaluate_sequence(lst))
       except Exception:
          return
       if np.isfinite(val) and val < best_score:
          best_score=val; best_list=lst; best_arr=arr.copy()
```

```
def _fourier_optimize(k, deadline, rng, max_evals=300):
   M=3; d=2*M+1
   x0 = rng.uniform(-0.5,0.5,size=d) maybe plus a=0? 
   def obj(theta):
      a=theta[:M]; b=theta[M:2*M]; skew=theta[2*M]
      t=(np.arange(k)+0.5)/k
      z=skew*(2*t-1)
      for m in range(1,M+1):
          z += a[m-1]*np.cos(2πm t) + b[m-1]*np.sin(2πm t)
      z = np.clip(z, -5,5)
      p=np.exp(z); p/=p.sum()
      bconv=_conv_full(p,p)
      mv=fast logsumexp: tau=max(1e-3*max(bconv),1e-8); smooth=tau*logsum(bconv/tau)
      return 2*k*smooth
   res=minimize(obj,x0,method='Nelder-Mead',options={'maxiter':300,'xatol':1e-4,'fatol':1e-7})
   p=...
   return p
```

```
def _fourier_optimize(k, deadline, rng):
    if minimize is None or k<16: return None
    M=3
    nvars=2*M+1
    trials=1 if (deadline-time.monotonic())<30 else 2
    best_p=None; best_obj=float('inf')
    for tr in range(trials):
       x0=rng.normal(0.0,0.35,size=nvars)
       # ensure some skew random maybe
       if tr==0: x0[-1]=0.3*rng.choice([-1,1])? rng.choice returns array? use sign.
       def obj(theta, _k=k):
          if time.monotonic() > deadline: raise _TimeStop
          a=theta[:M]; bb=theta[M:2*M]; skew=theta[2*M]
          t=(np.arange(_k)+0.5)/float(_k)
          z=skew*(2.0*t-1.0)
          x2=np.arange(_k)
          for m in range(1,M+1):
             ang=2.0*np.pi*m*x2/float(_k)
             z = z + a[m-1]*np.cos(ang) + bb[m-1]*np.sin(ang)
          z = z - z.mean()
          z = np.clip(z, -4.0, 4.0)
          p=np.exp(z); s=p.sum(); if s<=0: return 1e6
          p=p/s
          b=_conv_full(p)
          mb=float(np.max(b))
          if not np.isfinite(mb): return 1e6
          tau=max(0.02*mb,1e-8)
          shifted=np.exp((b-mb)/tau)
          smooth = mb + tau*float(np.log(np.sum(shifted)))
          return 2.0*float(_k)*smooth
       try:

           # compute exact p and actual score fast
           ...
       except _TimeStop:
           break
       except Exception:
           continue
       if best_p not None and ...
       if time>deadline break
    return best_p
```

```python
import numpy as np
import time

try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog
except Exception:
    linprog = None

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize as _scipy_minimize
except Exception:
    _scipy_minimize = None


class _TimeStop(Exception):
    pass


def _normalize(x):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if x.size == 0:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    x = np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
    x = np.maximum(x, 0.0)
    s = float(x.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.full(max(1, x.size), 1.0 / max(1, x.size), dtype=np.float64)
    return x / s


def _conv_full(a, b=None):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if b is None:
        b = a.copy()
    else:
        b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    la = a.size
    lb = b.size
    if la == 0 or lb == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    out_len = la + lb - 1

    # For all working sizes we keep below 4096; exact convolution is cheap
    # enough and avoids FFT surrogate issues.
    if max(la, lb) <= 2048:
        return np.convolve(a, b, mode="full")

    L = 1
    while L < out_len:
        L <<= 1
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    fb = np.fft.rfft(b, L)
    out = np.fft.irfft(fa * fb, L)[:out_len]
    return out.real


def _score_fast(q):
    q = np.asarray(q, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = q.size
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    b = _conv_full(q)
    if b.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    m = float(np.max(b))
    if not np.isfinite(m) or m < 0.0:
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * n * m)


def _build_a(qs):
    n = qs.size
    A = np.zeros((2 * n - 1, n), dtype=np.float64)
    qr = qs[::-1].copy()
    for i in range(n - 1):
        L = i + 1
        A[i, :L] = qr[-L:]
    for i in range(n - 1, 2 * n - 1):
        start = i - n + 1
        L = n - start
        A[i, start:start + L] = qr[:L]
    return A


def _lp_response(q, rng, end_time):
    if linprog is None:
        return None
    q = np.asarray(q, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = q.size
    if n == 0 or n > 1280:
        return None

    b = _conv_full(q)
    M0 = float(np.max(b)) if b.size else 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(M0) or M0 <= 0.0:
        return None

    qs = q * float(n)
    M = float(n) * M0
    A = _build_a(qs)

    # Tiny objective perturbation breaks degeneracies and makes repeated LPs
    # explore adjacent extreme points.
    c = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) - 1.0e-5 * qs
    try:
        c = c + rng.normal(0.0, 1.0e-9, size=n)
    except Exception:
        pass

    b_ub = np.full(A.shape[0], float(M), dtype=np.float64)
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0e6)] * n

    now = time.monotonic()
    tlim = float(max(0.3, min(15.0, end_time - now - 6.0)))
    if n <= 640:
        tlim = min(tlim, 8.0)

    res = None
    try:
        res = linprog(
            c,
            A_ub=A,
            b_ub=b_ub,
            bounds=bounds,
            method="highs",
            options={"presolve": True, "time_limit": tlim},
        )
    except Exception:
        try:
            res = linprog(
                c,
                A_ub=A,
                b_ub=b_ub,
                bounds=bounds,
                method="highs",
                options={"presolve": True},
            )
        except Exception:
            try:
                res = linprog(
                    c,
                    A_ub=A,
                    b_ub=b_ub,
                    bounds=bounds,
                    method="simplex",
                )
            except Exception:
                res = None

    if res is None or not getattr(res, "success", False):
        return None

    g = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
    g = np.where(np.isfinite(g) & (g > 0.0), g, 0.0)
    s = float(g.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return None
    g = g / s
    return g


def _mix_candidates(q, g):
    q = np.asarray(q, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    g = np.asarray(g, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    out = []

    for t in (0.02, 0.05, 0.1, 0.2, 0.35, 0.55, 0.8, 1.0):
        out.append(_normalize((1.0 - t) * q + t * g))

    qe = np.maximum(q, 1.0e-15)
    ge = np.maximum(g, 1.0e-15)
    for t in (0.1, 0.25, 0.45, 0.7, 1.0):
        out.append(_normalize(qe ** (1.0 - t) * ge ** t))

    dedup = []
    for x in out:
        if not isinstance(x, np.ndarray) or x.size == 0:
            continue
        same = False
        for y in dedup:
            if x.size == y.size and float(np.max(np.abs(x - y))) < 1.0e-14:
                same = True
                break
        if not same:
            dedup.append(x)
    return dedup


def _lp_refine(q, best_sc, rng, end_time, max_lp=60):
    q = _normalize(q)
    best_q = q.copy()
    best_sc = _score_fast(q)
    stag = 0
    it = 0
    eps = 1.0e-11 if q.size >= 1536 else 1.0e-12

    while it < max_lp and time.monotonic() < end_time - 5.0:
        it += 1
        g = _lp_response(q, rng, end_time)
        if g is None:
            break

        best_c = None
        best_c_sc = best_sc
        for cand in _mix_candidates(q, g):
            sc = _score_fast(cand)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_c_sc - eps:
                best_c_sc = sc
                best_c = cand

        if best_c is not None and best_c_sc < best_sc - eps:
            best_q = best_c.copy()
            best_sc = best_c_sc
            q = best_q.copy()
            stag = 0
        else:
            stag += 1
            if stag >= 5:
                break

    return best_q, best_sc


def _make_h(q, b, M, uniform=False, thresh=0.05, max_k=64, single=False):
    n = q.size
    if M <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(M):
        return None

    if single:
        act = np.array([int(np.argmax(b))], dtype=int)
    else:
        tol = max(float(thresh) * M, 1.0e-14)
        act = np.flatnonzero(b >= M - tol)
        if act.size > max_k:
            vals = -b[act]
            take = np.argpartition(vals, max_k - 1)[:max_k]
            act = act[take]
        if act.size == 0:
            return None

    if uniform or act.size == 1:
        w = np.ones(act.size, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        tau = max(0.15 * M, 1.0e-15)
        w = np.exp((b[act] - M) / tau)
    sw = float(w.sum())
    if sw <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(sw):
        w = np.ones(act.size, dtype=np.float64)
        sw = float(w.sum())
    w = w / sw

    h = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for idx, ww in zip(act, w):
        if ww <= 1.0e-14:
            continue
        i = int(idx)
        st = max(0, i - n + 1)
        en = min(n - 1, i)
        seg = q[i - en:i - st + 1][::-1]
        h[st:en + 1] += (2.0 * float(ww)) * seg

    h -= float(h.mean())
    nx = float(np.max(np.abs(h)))
    if not np.isfinite(nx) or nx <= 1.0e-15:
        return None
    return h / nx


def _candidates_for_eta(q, eta):
    q = np.asarray(q, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    eta = np.asarray(eta, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = q.size
    out = []

    if n <= 768:
        exps = (0.05, 0.25, 1.0)
        rels = (0.1, 0.5)
        adds = (1.0 / n, 10.0 / n)
    elif n <= 1536:
        exps = (0.05, 1.0)
        rels = (0.3,)
        adds = (1.0 / n, 5.0 / n)
    else:
        exps = (0.05, 1.0)
        rels = ()
        adds = (5.0 / n,)

    qsafe = np.maximum(q, 1.0e-15)
    for a in exps:
        out.append(_normalize(qsafe * np.exp(a * eta)))
    for a in rels:
        out.append(_normalize(q * (1.0 + a * eta)))
    for a in adds:
        out.append(_normalize(q + a * eta))

    dedup = []
    for x in out:
        if x is None or x.size == 0:
            continue
        same = False
        for y in dedup:
            if x.size == y.size and float(np.max(np.abs(x - y))) < 1.0e-14:
                same = True
                break
        if not same:
            dedup.append(x)
    return dedup


def _polish_step(q, q_sc):
    q = np.asarray(q, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = q.size
    b = _conv_full(q)
    if b.size == 0:
        return None
    M = float(np.max(b))
    if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
        return None

    dirs = []
    if n <= 1536:
        dirs.append(_make_h(q, b, M, uniform=False, thresh=0.05, max_k=64))
        dirs.append(_make_h(q, b, M, uniform=True, thresh=0.02, max_k=24))
    else:
        dirs.append(_make_h(q, b, M, uniform=True, thresh=0.03, max_k=16))
    dirs.append(_make_h(q, b, M, single=True))

    best_c = None
    best_s = float(q_sc)
    eps = 1.0e-10 if n >= 1536 else 1.0e-12

    for h in dirs:
        if h is None:
            continue
        for cand in _candidates_for_eta(q, h):
            sc = _score_fast(cand)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_s - eps:
                best_s = sc
                best_c = cand

    if best_c is not None:
        return best_c, best_s
    return None


def _perturb(q, scale, rng):
    q = np.asarray(q, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = q.size
    if n == 0:
        return q

    r = rng.standard_normal(n)
    if n >= 16:
        rf = np.fft.rfft(r)
        cut = max(2, int(0.15 * n))
        rf[cut:] *= 0.0
        r = np.fft.irfft(rf, n)

    r -= float(r.mean())
    rn = float(np.linalg.norm(r))
    if rn <= 1.0e-14:
        r = rng.standard_normal(n)
        r -= float(r.mean())
        rn = float(np.linalg.norm(r))
        if rn <= 1.0e-14:
            r = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
            r -= r.mean()
            rn = float(np.linalg.norm(r))
    if rn <= 1.0e-14:
        return q
    r = (r / rn) * float(scale)
    return _normalize(q + r)


def _polish(q, best_sc, deadline, rng):
    q = _normalize(q)
    q_sc = _score_fast(q)
    if q_sc < best_sc:
        best_sc = q_sc
    best_q = q.copy()

    perturbs = 0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 5.0:
        step = _polish_step(q, q_sc)
        if step is not None:
            q, q_sc = step
            if q_sc < best_sc - 1.0e-12:
                best_sc = q_sc
                best_q = q.copy()
            perturbs = 0
            continue

        if perturbs < 2 and time.monotonic() < deadline - 25.0:
            scale = (0.05 if perturbs == 0 else 0.10) / np.sqrt(max(1, q.size))
            p = _perturb(best_q, scale, rng)
            psc = _score_fast(p)
            perturbs += 1
            if np.isfinite(psc) and psc < best_sc - 1.0e-12:
                best_sc = psc
                best_q = p.copy()
                q = p
                q_sc = psc
                continue

        break

    return best_q, best_sc


def _resize(q, k, smooth=False):
    if q is None:
        return _normalize(np.ones(k, dtype=np.float64))
    q = np.asarray(q, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if k <= 0:
        k = 1
    if q.size == 0:
        return _normalize(np.ones(k, dtype=np.float64))
    if q.size == 1:
        return _normalize(np.full(k, q[0] if np.isfinite(q[0]) and q[0] > 0 else 1.0))

    if q.size == k:
        out = q.astype(np.float64, copy=True)
    else:
        xo = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, q.size, dtype=np.float64)
        xn = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, k, dtype=np.float64)
        out = np.interp(xn, xo, q)

    if smooth and out.size >= 3:
        w = np.array([1.0, 2.0, 1.0], dtype=np.float64)
        w /= w.sum()
        out = np.convolve(out, w, mode="same")

    return _normalize(out)


def _random_start(k, rng, variant=0):
    if k <= 0:
        k = 1
    t = (np.arange(k, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(k)
    raw = np.ones(k, dtype=np.float64)

    mmax = min(3, max(1, k // 20))
    for m in range(1, mmax + 1):
        ang = 2.0 * np.pi * m * t
        a1 = 0.80 if m == 1 else 0.45 / (float(m) ** 0.7)
        a2 = 0.50 if m == 1 else 0.35 / (float(m) ** 0.7)
        p1 = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        p2 = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        raw += a1 * np.sin(ang + p1)
        raw += a2 * np.cos(ang + p2)

    sk = 0.8 if variant % 2 else -0.8
    raw += sk * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * t)
    raw = np.maximum(raw, 1.0e-3)
    return _normalize(raw)


def _make_fourier_start(k, mode=0):
    t = (np.arange(k, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(k)
    if mode == 0:
        raw = 1.0 - 0.9 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * t) - 0.35 * np.sin(4.0 * np.pi * t)
        raw += 0.25 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * t)
    elif mode == 1:
        raw = 1.0 + 0.9 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * t) + 0.35 * np.sin(4.0 * np.pi * t)
        raw -= 0.25 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * t)
    else:
        raw = np.exp(0.9 * np.cos(np.pi * t) - 0.35 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * t))
    raw = np.maximum(raw, 1.0e-3)
    return _normalize(raw)


def _make_starts(k, best_q, parent, rng, max_starts=3):
    starts = []
    if parent is not None:
        starts.append(_resize(parent, k, smooth=False))
    if best_q is not None:
        starts.append(_resize(best_q, k, smooth=False))
        starts.append(_resize(best_q, k, smooth=True))
    if parent is not None:
        starts.append(_resize(parent, k, smooth=True))

    for v in range(2):
        starts.append(_make_fourier_start(k, mode=v))
    for v in range(2):
        starts.append(_random_start(k, rng, variant=v))

    uniq = []
    for q in starts:
        q = _normalize(q)
        if q is None or q.size != k:
            continue
        same = False
        for u in uniq:
            if u.size == q.size and float(np.max(np.abs(q - u))) < 1.0e-6:
                same = True
                break
        if not same:
            uniq.append(q)
            if len(uniq) >= max(1, int(max_starts)):
                break
    if not uniq:
        uniq = [_normalize(np.ones(max(1, int(k)), dtype=np.float64))]
    return uniq[:max(1, int(max_starts))]


def _fourier_optimize(k, deadline, rng):
    if _scipy_minimize is None or k < 32:
        return None

    k = int(k)
    M = 3
    nvars = 2 * M + 1

    idx = np.arange(k, dtype=np.float64)
    t = (idx + 0.5) / float(k)
    cos_s = []
    sin_s = []
    for m in range(1, M + 1):
        ang = 2.0 * np.pi * m * idx / float(k)
        cos_s.append(np.cos(ang))
        sin_s.append(np.sin(ang))

    def theta_to_p(theta):
        th = np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if th.size < nvars:
            th = np.pad(th, (0, nvars - th.size), mode="constant")
        else:
            th = th[:nvars]
        th = np.where(np.isfinite(th), th, 0.0)
        a = th[:M]
        b = th[M:2 * M]
        skew = float(th[2 * M])
        z = skew * (2.0 * t - 1.0)
        for j in range(M):
            z = z + a[j] * cos_s[j] + b[j] * sin_s[j]
        z = z - float(z.mean())
        z = np.clip(z, -4.0, 4.0)
        p = np.exp(z)
        s = float(p.sum())
        if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
            return None
        return p / s

    def obj(theta):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline:
            raise _TimeStop
        p = theta_to_p(theta)
        if p is None:
            return 1.0e6
        b = _conv_full(p)
        mb = float(np.max(b))
        if not np.isfinite(mb) or mb <= 0.0:
            return 1.0e6
        tau = max(0.02 * mb, 1.0e-12)
        shifted = np.exp((b - mb) / tau)
        sm = float(np.sum(shifted))
        if sm <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(sm):
            return 1.0e6
        return float(2.0 * k * (mb + tau * np.log(sm)))

    best_p = None
    best_sc = float("inf")
    trials = 2 if (deadline - time.monotonic()) > 55.0 else 1

    for tr in range(trials):
        x0 = rng.normal(0.0, 0.35, size=nvars)
        x0[-1] = (0.5 if rng.random() < 0.5 else -0.5)
        try:
            res = _scipy_minimize(
                obj,
                x0,
                method="Nelder-Mead",
                options={"maxiter": 220, "xatol": 1.0e-5, "fatol": 1.0e-8},
            )
            theta = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
        except _TimeStop:
            break
        except Exception:
            continue

        p = theta_to_p(theta)
        if p is None:
            continue
        sc = _score_fast(p)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_p = p
            consider_in_for = None  # placeholder unused; do not consider here
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 25.0:
            break

    return best_p


def _optimize_start(q0, k, rng, end_time, use_lp, max_lp=60):
    q0 = _normalize(q0)
    best_q = q0.copy()
    best_sc = _score_fast(q0)

    now = time.monotonic()
    remaining = max(0.0, float(end_time) - now)
    if remaining <= 10.0:
        return best_q, best_sc

    # Short local descent to clean the start.
    if remaining > 35.0:
        p1 = min(end_time - 40.0, now + min(max(5.0, 0.10 * remaining), 30.0))
        if p1 - now > 3.0:
            tmp, sc = _polish(best_q, best_sc, p1, rng)
            if sc < best_sc:
                best_q = tmp
                best_sc = sc

    # LP-based larger moves for moderate sizes.
    if use_lp and time.monotonic() < end_time - 45.0:
        now = time.monotonic()
        avail = max(0.0, float(end_time) - now - 35.0)
        if avail > 20.0:
            lp_budget = min(260.0, max(10.0, 0.50 * avail))
            lp_end = min(end_time - 25.0, now + lp_budget)
            if lp_end - now > 8.0:
                tmp, sc = _lp_refine(best_q, best_sc, rng, lp_end, max_lp=max_lp)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                    best_q = tmp
                    best_sc = sc

    # Final local polish.
    if time.monotonic() < end_time - 6.0:
        fin_end = end_time - max(5.0, 0.02 * max(0.0, end_time - time.monotonic()))
        tmp, sc = _polish(best_q, best_sc, fin_end, rng)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_q = tmp
            best_sc = sc

    return best_q, best_sc


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    try:
        seed = int(seed)
    except Exception:
        seed = 42
    try:
        budget_s = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget_s = 1300.0
    if not np.isfinite(budget_s) or budget_s < 5.0:
        budget_s = 1300.0

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    hard = t0 + max(5.0, budget_s)
    reserve = max(15.0, 0.025 * budget_s)
    deadline = hard - reserve
    if deadline <= t0 + 1.0:
        deadline = t0 + max(1.0, 0.5 * budget_s)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    best_list = None
    best_score = float("inf")
    best_arr = None

    def consider(seq):
        nonlocal best_list, best_score, best_arr
        if seq is None:
            return
        arr = _normalize(seq)
        if arr is None or arr.size == 0:
            return
        arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
        if float(arr.sum()) <= 0.0:
            return
        lst = [float(x) for x in arr]
        try:
            val = float(evaluate_sequence(lst))
        except Exception:
            return
        if np.isfinite(val) and val < best_score:
            best_score = val
            best_list = lst
            best_arr = arr.copy()

    parent = None
    try:
        p = height_sequence_1
        parent = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if parent.size == 0:
            parent = None
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    q4 = np.array([0.2, 0.4, 0.2, 0.2], dtype=np.float64)
    consider(parent)
    consider(q4)

    if budget_s < 45.0 or time.monotonic() > deadline - 15.0:
        if best_list is None:
            best_list = [float(x) for x in _normalize(q4)]
        try:
            v = float(evaluate_sequence(best_list))
            if not np.isfinite(v):
                best_list = [float(x) for x in _normalize(q4)]
        except Exception:
            best_list = [float(x) for x in _normalize(q4)]
        return [float(x) for x in best_list]

    if parent is not None and parent.size > 0:
        base = int(parent.size)
    else:
        base = 512
    if base < 32:
        base = 512
    if base > 2048:
        base = 2048

    lengths = []
    for mult in (1, 2, 4):
        kk = int(round(base * float(mult)))
        if kk < 32:
            kk = 32
        if kk > 2048:
            kk = 2048
        if kk not in lengths:
            lengths.append(kk)

    # Optional low-dimensional Fourier search: fast, cheap, and useful for
    # seeding a different basin before high-dimensional polishing.
    if budget_s > 260.0 and (deadline - time.monotonic()) > 180.0:
        for kf in (base, min(2 * base, 1024)):
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 120.0:
                break
            p4 = _fourier_optimize(kf, min(deadline - 90.0, time.monotonic() + 60.0), rng)
            if p4 is not None:
                consider(p4)

    max_times = [520.0, 380.0, 280.0]
    scale = max(0.05, min(1.0, max(0.0, budget_s - 60.0) / 1150.0))
    max_times = [t * scale for t in max_times]

    for li, k in enumerate(lengths):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 25.0:
            break
        mt = max_times[li] if li < len(max_times) else max(0.0, max_times[-1] * 0.6)
        end_pl = min(deadline - 12.0, time.monotonic() + mt)
        if end_pl - time.monotonic() < 20.0:
            break

        max_starts = 3 if li == 0 else 2
        starts = _make_starts(k, best_arr, parent, rng, max_starts=max_starts)
        n_starts = len(starts)

        for si, q0 in enumerate(starts):
            now = time.monotonic()
            if now > end_pl - 12.0:
                break
            rem_starts = max(1, n_starts - si)
            alloc = max(18.0, (end_pl - now - 5.0) / float(rem_starts))
            alloc = min(alloc, 320.0)
            if alloc < 9.0:
                break
            end_st = now + alloc

            use_lp = (k <= 1024) and li <= 1
            max_lp = 70 if k <= 640 else 30
            try:
                q_opt, _ = _optimize_start(q0, k, rng, end_st, use_lp, max_lp=max_lp)
            except Exception:
                q_opt = None
            if q_opt is not None:
                consider(q_opt)

    # Final polish of the current best array, if meaningful time remains.
    if best_arr is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline - 35.0:
        kk = int(best_arr.size)
        fin_alloc = max(20.0, min(320.0, 0.55 * max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic() - 25.0)))
        end_fin = min(deadline - 15.0, time.monotonic() + fin_alloc)
        use_lp = kk <= 1024
        max_lp = 25 if kk <= 1024 else 0
        try:
            q_fin, _ = _optimize_start(best_arr, kk, rng, end_fin, use_lp, max_lp=max_lp)
        except Exception:
            q_fin = None
        if q_fin is not None:
            consider(q_fin)

    if best_list is None:
        best_list = [float(x) for x in _normalize(q4)]

    # Final validation against the authoritative evaluator.
    try:
        v = float(evaluate_sequence(best_list))
        if not np.isfinite(v):
            raise ValueError
    except Exception:
        best_list = [float(x) for x in _normalize(q4)]
        try:
            evaluate_sequence(best_list)
        except Exception:
            pass

    return [float(x) for x in best_list]
```
